# MHA SP2 — Ultra Analysis evidence reconciliation records

This source partition belongs to the [canonical supplemental reconciliation ledger](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#records). It preserves the original evidence IDs and claim text. The index owns cross-book routing and cumulative progress; this file owns the detailed records for UAN. A storage partition is not an independent witness, another project entrypoint or an additional analytical conclusion.

## Ultra Analysis checkpoint

Each record inherits UAN edition/time/attribution limits from the [book audit](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_ULTRA_ANALYSIS_PARATEXT_AUDIT.md#edition-authorship-and-inherited-time-metadata). The record’s audience is the guidebook readership unless a depicted participant or direct addressee is specified. Unknown original item/revision dates remain unknown.

These are admitted UAN source records, with **current-home propagation pending the ordered three-book reconciliation**. Destination links identify the existing responsible homes and review scope; they do not certify that those files or tiers were revised at this checkpoint. A source-framing record may appropriately remain in this ledger/book audit with no behavioral propagation. “NEW” means a newly admitted claim/context at this corpus boundary, not a verified first-ever publication or independent behavioral observation.
